is paracontrolled to take advantage of the regularizing effect of
\cdot\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n}
. Starting with the
w^{2}
term, we get

|\langle w^{2}\mathbf{z}^{(1)},w^{3p-3}\rangle|\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w^{3p-1}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

since
\mathbf{z}^{(1)}
has spatial Hölder regularity
-\varepsilon
. Using the power product rule Lemma
B.1
and interpolation Lemma
B.2
in Besov spaces, we bound
w^{3p-1}
as

\displaystyle\|w^{3p-1}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w^{3p-2}\|_{L^{\frac{3p}{3p-2}}}\|w\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{2},\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-\frac{3}{2}}\|w\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}.

Using Hölder’s estimate, we get

\displaystyle\left|\int_{s}^{t}\langle w_{r}^{2}\mathbf{z}_{r}^{(1)},w_{r}^{3p-3}\rangle\mathrm{d}r\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{s}^{t}\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-\frac{3}{2}}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\sup_{0\leq r\leq t}\|\mathbf{z}_{r}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\Big(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\Big)^{\frac{3p-\frac{3}{2}}{3p}}\Big(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\Big)^{\frac{1}{2p}}

and thanks to the embedding
B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}\hookrightarrow L^{3p}
together with Young’s estimate, we have

\displaystyle\left|\int_{s}^{t}\langle w_{r}^{2}\mathbf{z}_{r}^{(1)},w_{r}^{3p-3}\rangle\mathrm{d}r\right|

\displaystyle\leq\int_{s}^{t}\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-\frac{3}{2}}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\delta\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r+\big(\sup_{0\leq r\leq t}\|\mathbf{z}_{r}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\big)^{p}c(\delta)\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|^{p}_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r

since
\mathbf{z}^{(1)}
is in
C_{t}\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}
. Turning to the
(v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n})^{2}
term, we make use of the regularizing effects of the operator
\cdot\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n}

\displaystyle\left|\langle(v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n})^{2}\mathbf{z}^{(1)},w^{3p-3}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|(v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n})^{2}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{\varepsilon}}\|w^{3p-3}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|v\|_{L^{3p}}^{2}\|w^{3p-3}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{2}\|w^{3p-3}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}.

As before, the power product and interpolation rules yield

\displaystyle\|w^{3p-3}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w^{3p-4}\|_{L^{\frac{3p}{3p-4}}}\|w\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{4},\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-4+\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{7},\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

and we conclude using once again Young estimate. Same goes for the mixed term
w(v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n})

\displaystyle\left|\langle w(v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n})\mathbf{z}^{(1)},w^{3p-3}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{\varepsilon}}\|w^{3p-2}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|v\|_{L^{3p}}\|w^{3p-2}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}\|w^{3p-2}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-2}\|w\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-\frac{3}{2}}\|w\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{5},\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

and we finally obtain that

\displaystyle\left|\int_{s}^{t}\left\langle v_{r}^{2}\mathbf{z}^{(1)},w_{r}^{3p-3}\right\rangle\mathrm{d}r\right|

\displaystyle\leq\delta\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|^{3p}_{L^{3p}}\mathrm{d}r+\big(\sup_{0\leq r\leq t}\|\mathbf{z}_{r}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\big)^{2p}c(\delta)\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|^{p}_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r.

The bound with the linear part or the constant part all follow from similar estimates, the only difference is that
\mathbf{z}^{(2)}
and
\mathbf{z}^{(3)}
are not continuous in time but rather
L^{q}
for any
q<+\infty
. This is however not an issue as the homogeneity in
w
in these terms is at most 1. For the linear terms, we get

\displaystyle\left|\langle(v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n})\mathbf{z}^{(2)},w^{3p-3}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{\varepsilon}}\|w^{3p-3}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|v\|_{L^{3p}}\|w^{3p-3}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}\|w^{3p-3}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-3}\|w\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{4},\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-\frac{5}{2}}\|w\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{5},\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}+c(\delta)\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\frac{6p}{5}}\|w\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p}{5}}

and

\displaystyle\left|\langle w\mathbf{z}^{(2)},w^{3p-3}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w^{3p-2}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-3}\|w\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-\frac{5}{2}}\|w\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq\delta\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}+c(\delta)\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\frac{6p}{5}}\|w\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p}{5}}.

Integrating over time and applying Young’s inequality, we obtain

\displaystyle\left|\int_{s}^{t}\left\langle v_{r}\mathbf{z}_{r}^{(2)},w_{r}^{3p-3}\right\rangle\mathrm{d}r\right|\leq\delta\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{3p}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r+c(\delta)\int_{0}^{t}\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}_{r}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\big(1+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r\big).

As for the linear term, this is basically the same idea, while being slightly easier as we do not need to use an interpolation inequality

\displaystyle\left|\langle\mathbf{z}^{(3)},w^{3p-3}\rangle\right|

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(3)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w^{3p-3}\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{1,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(3)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-4}\|w\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\delta\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}+c(\delta)\|\mathbf{z}^{(3)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\frac{3p}{4}}\|w\|_{B^{\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p}{4}}.

Altogether, we obtain

\left|\int_{s}^{t}\left\langle Q_{2}(v_{r},\mathbf{z}_{r}),w_{r}^{3p-3}\right\rangle\mathrm{d}r\right|\leq\delta\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r+c(\delta)K_{t}\big(1+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r).

\square

Lemma 3.8 –

For any
\delta>0
, there exists a constant
c>0
that depends only on
\delta,p,\varepsilon
such that for any
0<s<t<T
, we have

\left|\int_{s}^{t}\langle Q_{3}(v_{r},\mathbf{z}_{r}),w_{r}^{3p-3}\rangle\mathrm{d}r\right|\leq\delta\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r+cK_{t}\big(1+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B_{p,\infty}^{2\varepsilon}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\big)

where
K_{t}
is defined in (
3.18
).

Proof :

This is an easier counterpart of the previous proof as the regularizing operator
w\mapsto w\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n}
allows to estimate
w^{3p-3}
in
L^{\frac{3p}{3p-3}}
directly

\langle Q_{3}(v,\mathbf{z}),w^{3p-3}\rangle\lesssim\|v^{2}\mathbf{z}^{(1)}+v\mathbf{z}^{(2)}+\mathbf{z}^{(3)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p-3}

and the first term on the right-hand side is dealt with as before.

\square

All in all, we obtain the following first a priori bound for
w
taking
\delta<1
. In particular, it requires a regularity exponent strictly greater than one due to the roughness of the environment, the product with the space white noise
\xi\in\mathcal{C}^{-1-\kappa}
has to be well-defined.

Proposition 3.9 –

Let
p>\frac{4}{3\varepsilon}
be an even integer and
n
large enough depending on
p
. For
0<s<t<T
, we have

\displaystyle\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\|w_{s}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+K_{t}\big(1+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{1+\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\big)

where the implicit constant depends only on
p
and
\varepsilon
and
K_{t}>0
is given by (
3.18
).

Proof :

This is only a matter on putting together the estimates from Lemma
3.4
to
3.8
. Recall that testing the equation for
w
(
3.16
) against
w^{3p-3}
and integrating in time yields

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3p-2}\big(\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}-\|w_{s}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}\big)+(3p-3)\int_{s}^{t}\||\nabla w_{r}|^{2}w_{r}^{3p-4}\|_{L^{1}}\mathrm{d}r+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\hskip 28.45274pt=-\int_{s}^{t}\langle\xi\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to9.14pt{\vbox to9.14pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.57158pt\lower-4.57158pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.05 0 C 6.05 3.34 3.34 6.05 0 6.05 C -3.34 6.05 -6.05 3.34 -6.05 0 C -6.05 -3.34 -3.34 -6.05 0 -6.05 C 3.34 -6.05 6.05 -3.34 6.05 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.74626pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}w_{r},w_{r}^{3p-3}\rangle\mathrm{d}r-\int_{s}^{t}\langle Q(v_{r},w_{r},\mathbf{z}_{r}),w_{r}^{3p-3}\rangle\mathrm{d}r-\int_{s}^{t}\langle R_{n}(w_{r}),w_{r}^{3p-3}\rangle\mathrm{d}r

for any
0<s<t<T
. Note that the term involving
\nabla w
is nonnegative so that, using Hölder estimate on Lemma
3.4
and
3.5
as well as Lemma
3.6
,
3.7
,
3.8
we end up with

\displaystyle\frac{1}{3p-2}\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\leq\frac{1}{3p-2}\|w_{s}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\delta\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r+c_{\delta}K_{t}\big(1+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{1+\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\big)

for any
\delta>0
. Choosing
\delta
small enough, we can absorb the integrated
L^{3p}
-norm on the right hand side, hence the the estimate.

\square

3.3.  Higher regularity estimates on
w

As we intend to use a
coming down from infinity
argument, it remains to bound the rightmost term in order to close the estimate. This follows from the mild formulation of the equation.

Lemma 3.10 –

For any
0<s<t<T
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|^{p}_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim 1+\int_{s}^{t}\|e^{(r-s)\Delta}w_{s}\|^{p}_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r+\tilde{K}_{t}\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r

with
\tilde{K_{t}}>0
a constant of the form

(3.20)

\displaystyle{}\tilde{K}_{t}=\big(\sup_{0\leq r\leq t}\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}+t^{a}\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{L_{t}^{q}\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}+t^{a}\|\mathbf{z}^{(3)}\|_{L_{t}^{q}\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\big)^{b}

for exponents
a,b>0
and
q>1
all depending only on
p
and
\varepsilon
.

Remark 3.11 –

In the following, the exact exponents in
\tilde{K}
may differ from one line to the other, but we still write
\tilde{K}
for a constant having the same form as (
3.20
). Note that
\tilde{K}_{t}
depends on the randomness of
\mathbf{z}
only on the interval
[0,t]
, this will come in handy later on.

Proof :

Set
\gamma=1+4\varepsilon
and consider the mild formulation

w_{t}=e^{(t-s)\Delta}w_{s}-\int_{s}^{t}e^{(t-r)\Delta}\left(\xi\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to9.14pt{\vbox to9.14pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.57158pt\lower-4.57158pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.05 0 C 6.05 3.34 3.34 6.05 0 6.05 C -3.34 6.05 -6.05 3.34 -6.05 0 C -6.05 -3.34 -3.34 -6.05 0 -6.05 C 3.34 -6.05 6.05 -3.34 6.05 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.74626pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}w_{r}+R_{n}(w_{r})+w_{r}^{3}+Q(v_{r},w_{r},\mathbf{z}_{r})\right)\mathrm{d}r

to take advantage of the regularizing effects of the heat operator
e^{(t-r)\Delta}
. Provided
p
is large enough, and for any
\kappa<\frac{\gamma}{2}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\|e^{(t-r)\Delta}\big(\xi\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to9.14pt{\vbox to9.14pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.57158pt\lower-4.57158pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.05 0 C 6.05 3.34 3.34 6.05 0 6.05 C -3.34 6.05 -6.05 3.34 -6.05 0 C -6.05 -3.34 -3.34 -6.05 0 -6.05 C 3.34 -6.05 6.05 -3.34 6.05 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.74626pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}w_{r}\big)\|_{B^{\gamma}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{s}^{t}(t-r)^{-\frac{\gamma+\kappa}{2}}\|\xi\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to9.14pt{\vbox to9.14pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.57158pt\lower-4.57158pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 6.05 0 C 6.05 3.34 3.34 6.05 0 6.05 C -3.34 6.05 -6.05 3.34 -6.05 0 C -6.05 -3.34 -3.34 -6.05 0 -6.05 C 3.34 -6.05 6.05 -3.34 6.05 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.74626pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}w_{r}\|_{B_{p,\infty}^{-\kappa}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{s}^{t}(t-r)^{-\frac{\gamma+\kappa}{2}}\|w_{r}\|_{B_{p,\infty}^{1+2\kappa}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\Big(\int_{s}^{t}(t-r)^{-\frac{(\gamma+\kappa)p}{2(p-1)}}\mathrm{d}r\Big)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\Big(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B_{p,\infty}^{1+2\kappa}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\Big)^{\frac{1}{p}}

using Schauder estimates and Hölder inequality. We also get

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\|e^{(t-r)\Delta}R_{n}(w_{r})\|_{B^{\gamma}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{s}^{t}(t-r)^{-\frac{(\gamma+\kappa)p}{2(p-1)}}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{1+\kappa}_{p,\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

and for the cubic part

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\|e^{(t-r)\Delta}w_{r}^{3}\|_{B^{\gamma}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\left(\int_{s}^{t}(t-r)^{-\frac{\gamma p}{2(p-1)}}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

with similar arguments. Now for the collection of terms appearing in
Q
, recall that

(3.21)

Q_{1}(v,w)=-v^{3}\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n}+(v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n})^{3}+3(v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n})w^{2}+3(v\mathbin{\lx@scalerel@obj{\hbox to8.9pt{\vbox to8.9pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 4.45pt\lower-4.45pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {}{{}}{}{{}}{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\hbox{\hbox{{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{{}}}{{}}{}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}{{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 5.88 0 C 5.88 3.25 3.25 5.88 0 5.88 C -3.25 5.88 -5.88 3.25 -5.88 0 C -5.88 -3.25 -3.25 -5.88 0 -5.88 C 3.25 -5.88 5.88 -3.25 5.88 0 Z M 0 0}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
}{{{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@transformcm{1.0}{0.0}{0.0}{1.0}{-3.125pt}{-1.75pt}\lxSVG@begingroup@{transform=matrix(1.0 0.0 0.0 1.0 -4.32 -2.42)} \pgfsys@hbox{58}\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope }}}
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}}}X_{>n})^{2}w

gathers cubic homogeneity terms which can be bounded using Hölder inequality and Lemma
A.4
as

\displaystyle\|Q_{1}(w)\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|v^{3}\|_{L^{p}}+\|v\|_{L^{3p}}^{3}+\|v\|_{L^{3p}}\|w^{2}\|_{L^{\frac{3p}{2}}}+\|v\|_{L^{3p}}^{2}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3}

using the continuity of
\Gamma_{n}
hence

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\|e^{(t-r)\Delta}Q_{1}(v_{r},w_{r})\|_{B^{\gamma}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\left(\int_{s}^{t}(t-r)^{-\frac{\gamma p}{2(p-1)}}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{p-1}{p}}\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

integrating against
e^{(t-r)\Delta}
. Making use of the product rule Lemma
B.1
to handle the quadratic terms, we get

\displaystyle\|Q_{2}(v,\mathbf{z})\|_{B^{-\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|v\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{2},\infty}}\|v\|_{L^{3p}}+\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|v\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}+\|\mathbf{z}^{(3)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{2},\infty}}\|w\|_{L^{3p}}+\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}+\|\mathbf{z}^{(3)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}.

The analysis of the two last terms on the right-hand side is straightforward, we investigate the quadratic part. Integrating against
{e^{(t-r)\Delta}}
, we have

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\|e^{(t-r)\Delta}\mathbf{z}^{(1)}_{r}v_{r}^{2}\|_{B^{\gamma}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\int_{s}^{t}(t-r)^{-\frac{\gamma+\varepsilon}{2}}\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}_{r}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{2},\infty}}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\hskip 56.9055pt\lesssim\left(\int_{s}^{t}(t-r)^{-\frac{3(\gamma+\varepsilon)p}{2(3p-4)}}\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}_{r}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\frac{3p}{3p-4}}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{3p-4}{3p}}\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{2},\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{3p}}

using Hölder estimate. For
\varepsilon
small enough and
p
large enough, we have
\alpha_{1}:=\frac{3(1+5\varepsilon)p}{2(3p-4)}<1
and the
\mathbf{z}^{(1)}
integral can be treated as

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}(t-r)^{-\frac{3(\gamma+\varepsilon)p}{2(3p-4)}}\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}_{r}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\frac{3p}{3p-4}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq\frac{1}{1-\alpha_{1}}(t-s)^{1-\alpha_{1}}\sup_{0\leq r\leq t}\|\mathbf{z}_{r}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{\varepsilon}}^{\frac{3p}{3p-4}}.

Again, interpolation Lemma
B.2
between
L^{3p}
and
B^{4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}
yields

\|w\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{2}},\infty}\lesssim\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{\frac{1}{2}}\|w\|^{\frac{1}{2}}_{B^{4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}

thus we get

\displaystyle\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{2\varepsilon}_{\frac{3p}{2},\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{3p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{p}{2}}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{\frac{p}{2}}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{3p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p}{5}}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{5}{6p}}\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{2p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

using Young’s estimate. The very same argument can be used to deal with the lower homogeneity terms of
Q_{2}
and
Q_{3}
, although we make use of
\mathbf{z}^{(2)}
and
\mathbf{z}^{(3)}
being
L^{q}
in time for any
q<+\infty
to ensure that integrals of the type

\int_{s}^{t}(t-r)^{-\sigma}\|\mathbf{z}^{(i)}_{r}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}r

for
\sigma\in(0,1)
and
i\in\{1,2\}
are finite and uniformly bounded in
s
by some time integral of
\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}
and
\|\mathbf{z}^{(3)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}
. This proves that

\|w_{t}\|_{B^{\gamma}_{p,\infty}}\lesssim 1+\|e^{(t-s)\Delta}w_{s}\|_{B^{\gamma}_{p,\infty}}+\tilde{K}_{t}\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{1+\kappa}_{p,\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}+\tilde{K}_{t}\left(\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\right)^{\frac{1}{p}}

for any
0<\gamma<2
,
\kappa>0

p>1
large enough, where the implicit constant depends only on
\varepsilon,\kappa,p
. Since
\gamma=1+4\varepsilon
, it follows from interpolation and Young inequalities that

\displaystyle\|w\|_{B^{1+\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{1+4\varepsilon}p}\|w\|_{L^{p}}^{\frac{3\varepsilon}{1+4\varepsilon}p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{1+3\varepsilon}p}+\|w\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}.

Setting
\kappa=\varepsilon
and integrating over time finally yield

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|^{p}_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim 1+\int_{s}^{t}\|e^{(r-s)\Delta}w_{s}\|^{p}_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r+\tilde{K}_{t}\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{1+3\varepsilon}p}_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r.

Since
\frac{1+\varepsilon}{1+4\varepsilon}<1
, the last term on the right-hand side can be absorbed by the left-hand side using Young’s estimate, which concludes the proof.

\square

It only remains to turn the previous results into a proper integral inequality, this is the purpose of the following proposition.

Proposition 3.12 –

Let
p>\frac{4}{3\varepsilon}
be an even integer and
n
large enough depending on
p
. For
0<s<t<T
, we have

(3.22)

\displaystyle{}\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}\leq(c+\tilde{K}_{t})\Big(1+\|w_{s}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\|w_{s}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p-2}{3}}\Big)

and its integrated counterpart

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\left(\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\|w_{r}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p-2}{3}}\right)^{\frac{3p}{3p-2}}\mathrm{d}r\leq(c+\tilde{K}_{t})\Big(1+\|w_{s}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\|w_{s}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p-2}{3}}\Big)

for a positive constant
c=c(p,\varepsilon,X)>0
independent of time.

Proof :

We collect the two main ingredients provided by the previous Lemmas
3.9
and
3.10

\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim\|w_{s}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{1+\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r

and

\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|^{p}_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim 1+\int_{s}^{t}\|e^{(r-s)\Delta}w_{s}\|^{p}_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r.

Now set
\gamma:=(1+4\varepsilon)\frac{p-1}{p}
so that

\displaystyle\|w\|_{B^{\gamma}_{p,\infty}}^{p}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{p-1}\|w\|_{L^{p}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|w\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p-2}{3}}+\|w\|_{L^{p}}^{3p-2}.

From the estimate in Lemma
3.10
, we then get

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|^{p}_{B^{1+\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq c+\delta\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r+\delta\int_{s}^{t}\|e^{(r-s)\Delta}w_{s}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r

\displaystyle\leq c+\delta\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r+\delta\|w_{s}\|_{B^{\gamma}_{p,\infty}}^{p}

\displaystyle\leq c+\delta\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r+c(\delta)\big(\|w_{s}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p-2}{3}}+\|w_{s}\|_{L^{p}}^{3p-2}\big).

Pluging this in the right-hand side of Lemma
3.9
, we get

\displaystyle\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p}}^{3p}\mathrm{d}r+\int_{s}^{t}\|w_{r}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{p}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim 1+\|w_{s}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\|w_{s}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p-2}{3}}

where the implicit constant depends only on
\varepsilon,p
and
\mathbf{z}
which complete the proof. As each term on the left hand side is non negative, this yields

(3.23)

\displaystyle\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}\lesssim 1+\|w_{s}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\|w_{s}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p-2}{3}}

and

\displaystyle\int_{s}^{t}\left(\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\|w_{r}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p-2}{3}}\right)^{\frac{3p}{3p-2}}\mathrm{d}r\lesssim 1+\|w_{s}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\|w_{s}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p-2}{3}},

hence the estimate. Note that the implicit constants in these estimates only involve the noise terms
\mathbf{z}
through the use of all the previous lemmata, hence the particular form
c+\tilde{K}_{t}
.

\square

3.4.  Global well-posedness and estimates on the solution

Recall the following comparison result, Lemma
7.3
from Mourrat and Weber
[
17
]
. This lemma ensures that the behavior of
F
away from 0 is controlled uniformly in the initial condition, this is the so-called
coming down from infinity
.

Lemma 3.13 –

Let
\tau>s\geq 0
and
F:[s,\tau)\to\mathbb{R}_{+}
be continuous such that for any
s\leq s_{1}<s_{2}<\tau
, we have

\int_{s_{1}}^{s_{2}}F^{\lambda}(r)\mathrm{d}r\leq cF(s_{1})

for fixed
\lambda>1
and
c>0
. Then there exists
N\in\mathbb{N}^{*}
and
s=t_{0}<t_{1}<\cdots<t_{N}=\tau
such that

F(t_{n})\lesssim c^{\frac{1}{\lambda-1}}(t_{n+1}-s)^{-\frac{1}{\lambda-1}}

for
0\leq n\leq N-1
where the implicit constant depends only on
\lambda
.

Proof :

We construct the sequence
t_{0},\cdots,t_{N}
by induction. Define
t_{0}=s
and

t_{1}^{*}=t_{0}+c2^{\lambda}F(t_{0})^{1-\lambda},

if
t_{1}^{*}\geq\tau
then set
N=1
and
t_{1}=\tau
so that

F(t_{0})=F(s)=(t_{1}^{*}-s)^{-\frac{1}{\lambda-1}}c^{\frac{1}{\lambda-1}}2^{\frac{\lambda}{\lambda-1}}\lesssim(t_{1}-s)^{-\frac{1}{\lambda-1}}c^{\frac{1}{\lambda-1}}.

Otherwise, taking
s_{1}=s
and
s_{2}=t_{1}^{*}
, we have

(t_{1}^{*}-s)\min_{s\leq r\leq t_{1}^{*}}F^{\lambda}(r)\leq\int_{s}^{t_{1}^{*}}F^{\lambda}(r)\mathrm{d}r\leq cF(s)

thus

\min_{s\leq r\leq t_{1}^{*}}F^{\lambda}(r)\leq 2^{-\lambda}F^{\lambda}(s)

and choose
t_{1}
as

t_{1}=\inf\Argmin_{[t_{0},t_{1}^{*}]}F

so that in the end

F(t_{1})\leq\frac{1}{2}F(t_{0})\quad\text{and}\quad t_{1}-t_{0}\leq c2^{\lambda}F^{1-\lambda}(t_{0}).

Assume that
t_{0}<t_{1}<\cdots<t_{n}<\tau
have been constructed as above and set

t_{n+1}^{*}=t_{n}+c2^{\lambda}F^{1-\lambda}(t_{n}).

Either
t_{n+1}^{*}\geq\tau
, in which case we can set
N=n+1
and
t_{n+1}=\tau
and conclude, or
t_{n+1}^{*}<\tau
and we set

t_{n+1}=\inf\Argmin_{[t_{n},t_{n+1}^{*}]}F

so that, similarly to the initial step,

(3.24)

{}F(t_{n+1})\leq\frac{1}{2}F(t_{0})\quad\text{and}\quad t_{n+1}-t_{n}\leq c2^{\lambda}F^{1-\lambda}(t_{n}).

Note that the construction procedure terminates after a finite number of steps, indeed, by construction

t_{n+1}^{*}-t_{n}=c2^{\lambda}F(t_{n})^{1-\lambda}\geq 2^{\lambda-1}(t_{n}^{*}-t_{n-1})

thanks to the first part of (
3.24
), thus the time step is increased by a factor
2^{\lambda-1}>1
at each step of the procedure, thus
t_{n+1}
necessarily exceeds
\tau
after a finite number of steps.

As for the estimate, it suffices to write the increments as a telespoci sum

t_{n+1}-s=\sum_{k=0}^{n}(t_{k+1}-t_{k})\leq c2^{\lambda}\sum_{k=0}^{n}F^{1-\lambda}(t_{k})

where the last estimate follows by construction. Moreover, iterating the first part of (
3.24
) and recallig that
1-\lambda<0
, we get

F^{1-\lambda}(t_{k})\leq 2^{(1-\lambda)}F^{1-\lambda}(t_{k+1})\leq 2^{(1-\lambda)(n-k)}F^{1-\lambda}(t_{n})

for any
k\leq n
. Thus

\displaystyle t_{n+1}-s

\displaystyle=c2^{\lambda}F^{1-\lambda}(t_{n})\sum_{k=0}^{n}2^{(1-\lambda)(n-k)}

\displaystyle\leq c2^{\lambda}F^{1-\lambda}(t_{n})\sum_{k\geq 0}2^{k(1-\lambda)}

where the last series is convergent. Thus

F(t_{n})\lesssim c^{\frac{1}{\lambda-1}}(t_{n+1}-s)^{-\frac{1}{\lambda-1}}

where the implicit constant depends only on
\lambda
and the proof is complete.

\square

Proposition 3.14 –

Let
p>\frac{4}{3\varepsilon}
be an even integer and
n
large enough. We have

(1\wedge\sqrt{t})\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}\leq C\wedge\tilde{K}_{t}\leq C\wedge\tilde{K}_{T}

for all
0<t<T
. Moreover, the right-hand side does not depend on
u_{0}
and only depends on
\mathbf{z}
on the time interval
[0,T]
.

Proof :

Let
0<s<t<T
and consider

F:r\in[s,T)\mapsto\|w_{r}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}+\|w_{r}\|_{B^{1+4\varepsilon}_{p,\infty}}^{\frac{3p-2}{3}}

which satisfies the assumptions of Lemma
3.13
, this is the a priori estimates from Proposition
3.12
. Define also

\tau:=\inf\left\{r\geq s,F(r)\leq 1\right\}\wedge\,T

so that
F(r)\geq 1
for any
r\in[s,\tau]
. If
\tau\geq t
, then applying Lemma
3.13
, we obtain
s=t_{0}<\cdots<t_{N+1}=\tau
such that

F(t_{n})\lesssim(t_{n+1}-s)^{-\frac{3p-2}{2}}

since
\lambda=\frac{3p}{3p-2}>1
. Let
n
be such that
t_{n}\leq t<t_{n+1}
, then using (
3.22
), we get

(3.25)

\displaystyle\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}

\displaystyle\lesssim 1+F(t_{n})

(3.26)

\displaystyle\lesssim F(t_{n})

(3.27)

\displaystyle\lesssim(t_{n+1}-s)^{-\frac{3p-2}{2}}

(3.28)

\displaystyle\lesssim(t-s)^{-\frac{3p-2}{2}}

since
t_{n}\leq t\leq\tau
. When
\tau<t
, note that the previous case ensure that

\|w_{\tau}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}\lesssim(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3p-2}{2}},

so that applying (
3.22
) again, we get

\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}^{3p-2}\lesssim 1+F(\tau)\lesssim(\tau-s)^{-\frac{3p-2}{2}}.

Taking
s=\frac{t}{2}
, we finally obtain

(1\wedge\sqrt{t})\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}\leq C\vee\tilde{K}_{t}\leq C\vee\tilde{K}_{T}

for
0<t<T
where the dependency of the upper-bound comes from the fact that the
\mathbf{z}
terms in
\tilde{K}_{t}
only take into account what happens between the initial time and the current time, being 0 and
t
respectively.

\square

With the embedding
L^{3p-2}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}
for
p>\frac{4}{3\varepsilon}
and the equivalence of the norms
\|v_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}
and
\|w_{t}\|_{L^{3p-2}}
, this is enough to prove global well-posedness. Recall that the constant
\widetilde{K}_{T}>0
is defined by

(3.29)

\widetilde{K}_{T}=\big(\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}+t^{a}\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}\|_{L_{t}^{q}\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}+t^{a}\|\mathbf{z}^{(3)}\|_{L_{t}^{q}\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\big)^{b}

and almost surely finite for any
T>0
.

Corollary 3.15 –

Let
0<\varepsilon<\frac{1}{3}
and
u_{0}\in\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}
. Consider
v
the solution to

\displaystyle\begin{cases}\partial_{t}v+\mathcal{H}v+v^{3}+3v^{2}\mathbf{z}^{(1)}+3v\mathbf{z}^{(2)}+\mathbf{z}^{(3)}=0\\
v_{|t=0}=u_{0}\end{cases}

obtained in Proposition
3.1
and
T>0
the maximal time of existence. We have

(3.30)

(1\wedge\sqrt{t})\|v(t)\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\leq 1+\widetilde{K}_{T}

for any
0\leq t<T
. In particular, this implies that
T=+\infty
thus the solution is global.

Proof :

The estimate follows directly from Proposition
3.14
applied with the enhanced data
\mathbf{z}
, together with Lemma
3.3
and the embedding
L^{3p-2}\hookrightarrow\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}
for
p
large enough. The fact that the solution is global is then a consequence of the blow-up criterion in the local well-posedness theory
3.1
.

\square

To conclude this section, we give a number of probabilistic bounds on the solutions that follow from the previous estimates. This will be important in the following section to study the solution as an infinite dimensional stochastic process.

Corollary 3.16 –

For any
0<\varepsilon<\frac{1}{3}
,
u_{0}\in\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}
and
p\geq 1
, there exists positive constants
C,\gamma
such that

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{0\leq t\leq 1}\|v(t)\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{p}\right]\leq C(1+\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}})^{\gamma}.

Proof :

Let
T
be the time obtained by the fixed point argument in
3.1

T=c\left(\frac{\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}}{(1+\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}+\|\mathbf{z}\|_{L^{q}\big((0,1);\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}\big)^{3}})^{3}}\right)^{-\frac{1}{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}-3\frac{\sigma+\varepsilon}{2}}}

for some constant
c>0
and

t^{*}=1\wedge T\wedge c(1+\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}})^{-\frac{2}{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}-3\frac{\sigma+\varepsilon}{2}}}.

Then, for
0<t<t^{*}
, the fixed point bound (
3.8
) yields

\|v(t)\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}\leq C(1+\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}).

For
t^{*}\leq t\leq 1
, we use Proposition
3.14
to get

\displaystyle\|v(t)\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\leq\big(1\vee{t^{*}}^{-\frac{1}{2}}\big)\tilde{K}_{1}

\displaystyle\leq c^{-\frac{1}{2}}(1+\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}})^{\frac{1}{\frac{1}{q^{\prime}}-3\frac{\sigma+\varepsilon}{2}}}\tilde{K}_{1}

and we conclude by gluing the estimates on
[0,t^{*}]
and
[t^{*},1]
together, taking the
p
-th power and the expectation, using that
\tilde{K}_{1}
has finite moments.

\square

Note that another choice of ansatz for the solution
u
to (
1.1
) can be made

u=\tilde{v}+\hbox to3.38pt{\vbox to8.09pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 1.59164pt\lower-0.4pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{}}{}
{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.8pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L 0 8.3}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{{{}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.8pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@fillstroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0.14 8.3 M 1.93 8.3 C 1.93 9.29 1.13 10.09 0.14 10.09 C -0.85 10.09 -1.65 9.29 -1.65 8.3 C -1.65 7.32 -0.85 6.51 0.14 6.51 C 1.13 6.51 1.93 7.32 1.93 8.3 Z M 0.14 8.3}{} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope }
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}+e^{-t\mathcal{H}}u_{0}

so that
\tilde{v}
satisfies equation (
3.7
) with null initial condition and shifted noise data
\tilde{\mathbf{z}}=(\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)},\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(2)},\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(3)})
with

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)}

\displaystyle:=3\mathbf{z}^{(1)}+3e^{-t\mathcal{H}}u_{0},

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(2)}

\displaystyle:=3\mathbf{z}^{(2)}+6e^{-t\mathcal{H}}u_{0}\mathbf{z}^{(1)}+3\big(e^{-t\mathcal{H}}u_{0}\big)^{2},

\displaystyle\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(3)}

\displaystyle:=\mathbf{z}^{(3)}+3(e^{-t\mathcal{H}}u_{0})^{2}\mathbf{z}^{(1)}+3e^{-t\mathcal{H}}u_{0}\mathbf{z}^{(2)}+\big(e^{-t\mathcal{H}}u_{0}\big)^{3}

and falls under the scope of Proposition
3.1
and
3.15
. The main interest of
\tilde{v}
is that it has better regularity since
\tilde{v}(0)=0
is of better regularity than
u_{0}\in\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}
. Of course, one has to be careful here since the data
\tilde{\mathbf{z}}
depends on the initial data, this will be useful to prove Corollary
4.8
.

Lemma 3.17 –

Let
0<\varepsilon<\frac{1}{3}
,
u_{0}\in\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}
and
\tilde{v}
be the solution to

(3.31)

\displaystyle{}\begin{cases}\partial_{t}\tilde{v}+\mathcal{H}\tilde{v}+\mathbf{f}(\tilde{v},\tilde{\mathbf{z}})=0\\
\tilde{v}_{|t=0}=0.\end{cases}

For any
p\geq 1
, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{0\leq t\leq 1}\left(\|\tilde{v}(t)\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\|\tilde{v}(s)\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\right)^{p}\right]\leq C(1+\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}})^{\gamma}

for some constants
C,\gamma>0
depending only on
\varepsilon
and
p
.

Proof :

For
t>0
, define the energy

\Psi(t):=\frac{1}{2}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{2}+\frac{1}{2}\int_{0}^{t}\|\tilde{v}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s.

Note that running the same fixed-point argument as in Proposition
3.1
in
L^{2}
based Sobolev spaces ensures that
\Psi
is differentiable in time, with derivative given by

\displaystyle\Psi^{\prime}(t)

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{2}-\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{4}}^{4}-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\tilde{v}_{t}^{3}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)}_{t}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\tilde{v}_{t}^{2}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(2)}_{t}\mathrm{d}x-\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\tilde{v}_{t}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(3)}_{t}\mathrm{d}x.

Note that this is essentially the same as testing the equation
3.31
against
\tilde{v}
. It remains to bound the cross product terms to control them with the
\mathcal{D}^{1}
and
L^{4}
norms of
\tilde{v}
. Note that each integral can be bounded as a
\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon},W^{\varepsilon,1}
duality pairing with

\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\tilde{v}_{t}^{j+1}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(3-j)}_{t}\mathrm{d}x\right|\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}^{j+1}\|_{W^{\varepsilon,1}}\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(3-j)}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}

where
j\in\{0,1,2\}
. For
\eta\in(0,1)
small enough, interpolation and Young inequalities yield

\displaystyle\|\tilde{v}_{t}^{j+1}\|_{W^{\varepsilon,1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}^{j+1}_{t}\|_{W^{1-\eta,1}}^{\frac{\varepsilon}{1-\eta}}\|\tilde{v}^{j+1}_{t}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1-\eta-\varepsilon}{1-\eta}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}^{j+1}_{t}\|_{W^{1-\eta,1}}^{\frac{\varepsilon}{1-\eta}r}+\|\tilde{v}^{j+1}_{t}\|_{L^{1}}^{\frac{1-\eta-\varepsilon}{1-\eta}r^{\prime}}

where
r\geq 1
will be chosen later and
\frac{1}{r}+\frac{1}{r^{\prime}}=1
. Using the embedding
H^{1-\eta}\hookrightarrow W^{1-\eta,1}
and
L^{4}\hookrightarrow L^{j}
for
j\in\{0,1,2\}
, the power product can be bounded using Lemma
B.4
to get

\displaystyle\|\tilde{v}_{t}^{j+1}\|_{W^{\varepsilon,1}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{H^{1-\frac{\eta}{2^{j}}}}^{(j+1)\frac{\varepsilon}{1-\eta}r}+\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{j+1}}^{(j+1)\frac{1-\eta-\varepsilon}{1-\eta}r^{\prime}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{(j+1)\frac{\varepsilon}{1-\eta}r}+\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{4}}^{(j+1)\frac{1-\eta-\varepsilon}{1-\eta}r^{\prime}}.

We need to tune the exponents so that the
\mathcal{D}^{1}
norm (resp.
L^{4}
norm) is at most to the power
2
(resp.
4
), hence the following conditions

(j+1)\frac{\varepsilon}{1-\eta}r<2\qquad\text{and}\qquad(j+1)\frac{1-\eta-\varepsilon}{1-\eta}r^{\prime}<4

for
j\in\{0,1,2\}
. The second one happens as soon as
r>3
, i.e.
r^{\prime}<\frac{4}{3}
, and the first one follows by taking
\varepsilon
small enough. Hence, using again Young’s inequality, we have

\left|\int_{\mathbb{T}^{2}}\tilde{v}_{t}^{j+1}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(3-j)}_{t}\mathrm{d}x\right|\leq C_{1}(\delta)\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(3-j)}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{q_{1}}+\delta^{q_{1}^{\prime}}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{2}+C_{2}(\delta)\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(3-j)}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{q_{2}}+\delta^{q_{2}^{\prime}}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{4}}^{4}

for any
\delta>0
and

\frac{1}{q_{1}^{\prime}}=\frac{(j+1)r}{2}\frac{\varepsilon}{1-\eta}\qquad\text{and}\qquad\frac{1}{q_{2}^{\prime}}=\frac{(j+1)r^{\prime}}{4}\frac{1-\eta-\varepsilon}{1-\eta}.

Taking
\delta
small enough ensures that the cross product terms can be absorbed so that

\Psi^{\prime}(t)\lesssim\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\tilde{q}_{1}}+\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(2)}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\tilde{q}_{2}}+\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(3)}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\tilde{q}_{3}}

for some exponents
\tilde{q}_{1},\tilde{q}_{2},\tilde{q}_{3}\geq 1
. Since
\Psi(0)=0
, integration in time yields

\sup_{0\leq t\leq 1}\Psi(t)\lesssim\int_{0}^{1}\left(\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\tilde{q}_{1}}+\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(2)}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\tilde{q}_{2}}+\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(3)}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\tilde{q}_{3}}\right)\mathrm{d}s

and taking the
p
-th moment, the estimate follows from the fact that
\tilde{z}\in L^{p}\Big(\Omega;L^{q}\big((0,1);\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}\big)\Big)
for any
p,q\geq 1
.

\square

This in particular implies that
\tilde{v}
has positive spatial Sobolev regularity, uniformly in time.

Proposition 3.18 –

Let
0<\varepsilon<\sigma
be small enough,
u_{0}\in\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}
and
p\geq 1
. We have

\mathbb{E}\left[\sup_{0\leq t\leq 1}\|\tilde{v}(t)\|_{H^{\sigma}}^{p}\right]\leq C(1+\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\gamma})

for some
C,\gamma>0
depending only on
\varepsilon,\sigma
and
p
.

Proof :

Write the mild formulation for
\tilde{v}

\tilde{v}_{t}=-\int_{0}^{t}e^{-(t-s)\mathcal{H}}\left(\tilde{v}_{s}^{3}+\tilde{v}_{s}^{2}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)}_{s}+\tilde{v}_{s}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{(2)}+\tilde{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{(3)}\right)\mathrm{d}s.

Taking the
H^{\sigma}
norm inside and using Schauder estimates for
\mathcal{H}
, this reads

\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{H^{\sigma}}\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}(t-s)^{-\frac{\sigma+\varepsilon}{2}}\left(\|\tilde{v}_{s}^{3}\|_{H^{-\varepsilon}}+\|\tilde{v}_{s}^{2}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)}_{s}\|_{H^{-\varepsilon}}+\|\tilde{v}_{s}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{(2)}\|_{H^{-\varepsilon}}+\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}_{s}^{(3)}\|_{H^{-\varepsilon}}\right)\mathrm{d}s.

We now handle each term seperately using the embedding
H^{\sigma+\frac{1}{2}}\hookrightarrow B^{\sigma}_{4,4}
and interpolation in Besov spaces. We have

\displaystyle\|\tilde{v}_{t}^{3}\|_{H^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}^{2}\|_{H^{\sigma}}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{H^{\sigma+\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{H^{1-\delta}}^{\frac{2\sigma+1}{1-\delta}}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2-2\delta-(2\sigma+1)}{1-\delta}}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{\frac{2\sigma+1}{1-\delta}}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2-2\delta-(2\sigma+1)}{1-\delta}}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}

for any
0<\delta<\frac{1}{2}-\sigma
using the embedding
\mathcal{D}^{1}\hookrightarrow H^{1-\delta}
. Set
\delta=\frac{1-4\sigma}{3}
so that
\frac{2\sigma+1}{1-\delta}=\frac{3}{2}
. Young’s inequality then yields

\|\tilde{v}_{t}^{3}\|_{H^{-\varepsilon}}\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{\frac{7}{4}}+\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}+\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}^{\beta}

for some exponents
\alpha,\beta>0
. Note that the exact form of
\alpha,\beta
do not matter as the
L^{2}
and
\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}
norms of
\tilde{v}
are handled uniformly in time by Corollary
3.16
and Lemma
3.17
, our concern is on the exponent on the
\mathcal{D}^{1}
norm which has to be less than
2
as we can only bound its
L^{2}
norm in time. Integrating in time and using Hölder inequality, the first term yields

\displaystyle\int_{0}^{t}(t-s)^{-\frac{\sigma+\varepsilon}{2}}\|\tilde{v}_{t}^{3}\|_{H^{-\varepsilon}}\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim\int_{0}^{t}(t-s)^{-\frac{\sigma+\varepsilon}{2}}\left(\|\tilde{v}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{\frac{7}{4}}+\|\tilde{v}_{s}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}+\|\tilde{v}_{s}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}^{\beta}\right)\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\lesssim\left(\int_{0}^{t}\|\tilde{v}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\right)^{\frac{7}{8}}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq 1}\big(\|\tilde{v}_{s}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}+\|\tilde{v}_{s}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}^{\beta}\big)

provided
\sigma+\varepsilon<\frac{1}{4}
so that every time integral is bounded uniformly in
t\in[0,1]
.
A similar treatment allows to deal with the square term with

\displaystyle\|\tilde{v}_{t}^{2}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)}_{t}\|_{H^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{H^{\sigma+\frac{1}{2}}}^{2}\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{H^{1-\delta}}^{\frac{2\sigma+1}{1-\delta}}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2-2\delta-(2\sigma+1)}{1-\delta}}\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{\frac{2\sigma+1}{1-\delta}}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{\frac{2-2\delta-(2\sigma+1)}{1-\delta}}\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{\frac{7}{4}}+\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}+\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(1)}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}^{\beta}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{\frac{7}{4}}+\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{\alpha}+\|\mathbf{z}^{(1)}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}^{\beta}+\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\beta}

and the linear one is more straightforward

\displaystyle\|\tilde{v}_{t}\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(2)}_{t}\|_{H^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{H^{\sigma}}\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(2)}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(2)}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{2}+\|\tilde{\mathbf{z}}^{(2)}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}^{2}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}^{2}+t^{-(\sigma+\varepsilon)}\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{2}\|\mathbf{z}_{t}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{2}+t^{-2(\sigma+\varepsilon)}\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{4}

\displaystyle\lesssim\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{z}^{(2)}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}^{2}+\|\mathbf{z}_{t}^{(1)}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{4}+t^{-2(\sigma+\varepsilon)}\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{4}.

Integrating in time and using Hölder inequality as before, this yields

\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{H^{\sigma}}\lesssim\left(\int_{0}^{1}\|\tilde{v}_{s}\|_{\mathcal{D}^{1}}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\right)^{\alpha}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq 1}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{L^{2}}^{\beta}+\sup_{0\leq t\leq 1}\|\tilde{v}_{t}\|_{C^{-\varepsilon}}^{\gamma}+\|\mathbf{z}\|_{L^{q}((0,1);\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon})^{3}}+\|u_{0}\|_{\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}}^{\eta}

for some exponents
\alpha,\beta,\gamma,q
large enough. The conclusion then follows using Corollary
3.16
and Lemma
3.17
for the first three terms, and finiteness of the moments of
\|\mathbf{z}\|_{L^{q}((0,1);\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon})^{3}}
.

\square

Remark 3.19 –

Note that
3.15
,
3.16
,
3.18
and are stated for the solution to the limiting equation, but they all hold true for the renormalized solution
v_{N}
and
\tilde{v}_{N}
, with the right-hand side being uniform in
N
.

4.  Strong Feller property for Anderson
\Phi^{4}_{2}
model

We put the previous estimates to use to investigate the properties of the transition semigroup of equation (
1.1
)

\displaystyle P_{t}\varphi(u_{0}):=\mathbb{E}\Big[\varphi\big(u(t\hskip 1.42271pt;u_{0})\big)\Big]

where
u(t\hskip 1.42271pt;u_{0})
denote the solution at time
t
starting from
u_{0}\in\mathcal{C}^{-\varepsilon}
. We proved in the previous section that solutions are global, we work here for any time
t\geq 0
. In this section, it is convenient to consider

(4.1)

\hbox to3.38pt{\vbox to8.09pt{\pgfpicture\makeatletter\hbox{\hskip 1.59164pt\lower-0.4pt\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke=#000000} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.4pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces\nullfont\hbox to0.0pt{\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} {{}{{}}{}
{{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}}{}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.8pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@stroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0 0 L 0 8.3}{fill:none} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{{}}{}{{{}}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{}{}{}{}{}}\lxSVG@begingroup@{_scopebegin=1} \lxSVG@begingroup@{fill=#000000} \lxSVG@setlinewidth{\the\pgflinewidth}\lxSVG@begingroup@{stroke-width=0.8pt} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces{}\lxSVG@fillstroke\lxSVG@drawpath@unclipped{M 0.14 8.3 M 1.93 8.3 C 1.93 9.29 1.13 10.09 0.14 10.09 C -0.85 10.09 -1.65 9.29 -1.65 8.3 C -1.65 7.32 -0.85 6.51 0.14 6.51 C 1.13 6.51 1.93 7.32 1.93 8.3 Z M 0.14 8.3}{} \lx@inpgf@ignorespaces
\lxSVG@closescope }
\lxSVG@closescope {\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}{\lx@inpgf@ignorespaces}\hss}\lxSVG@discardpath\lxSVG@closescope \hss}}\lxSVG@closescope\endpgfpicture}}_{s,t}=\sqrt{2}\int_{s}^{t}e^{-(t-r)\mathcal{H}}\zeta(\mathrm{d}r)

for
s,t\geq 0
to use that the independence of time increments of
\zeta
as well as Markov property, we use the same notation for
\mathbf{z}=(\mathbf{z}^{(1)},\mathbf{z}^{(2)},\mathbf{z}^{(3)})
. We begin this section with preleminary results that will be important in the following.

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\int_{0}^{t+h}e^{-(t+h-r)\mathcal{H}}\zeta(\mathrm{d}r)-\sqrt{2}\int_{0}^{t}e^{-(t+h-r)\mathcal{H}}\zeta(\mathrm{d}r)

(4.4)

\displaystyle=\sqrt{2}\int_{t}^{t+h}e^{-(t+h-r)\mathcal{H}}\zeta(\mathrm{d}r)
